# Drop-in audit: is dash a drop-in replacement for twoway?

Audited 19oct2026 on s3-bugs 7dced3a (StataNow 19.5 MP, macOS arm64). 21 area auditors (15 planned, 6 added by a completeness critic), each followed by an adversarial verifier. 403 findings survived verification (367 confirmed, 33 partly, 3 uncertain; none refuted). Read-only: nothing in the repository was changed.

Revised 20oct2026 with the maintainer's decisions (DECISIONS.md D40). The items D40 withdraws are removed from this report. Nothing has been programmed: this document is input to a later planning process.

## Maintainer decisions (D40, 20oct2026)

These rulings govern every package below. Where a package says "no ruling", the maintainer has not decided it yet and the item stays open for planning.

- **heatmap and sunflower are not twoway plot types.** `heatmap` is not a Stata command, and sunflower plots are not a twoway graph type. No plan, audit or document may list either as a twoway plot type or as a gap in dash. Contour's own `heatmap` option is a different thing and stays in scope. The audit raised them because its prompt named them, and its auditors built the plot-type list from file names under StataNow's ado/base/t folder. dash.sthlp:737-742 also calls sunflower a twoway plot type. Both causes are corrected: the agent memory now forbids it, and the help sentence is listed below as a fix.
- **No programming yet.** These decisions update the documentation only. Implementation waits for a later planning process.
- **Planning order:** package 13 first, then the order in the table below.

| Package | Severity | Maintainer decision (D40) | What that means for planning |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Default drawing of common graphs | blocker | No ruling in D40. | Open. The standing rule (draw what twoway draws) applies; to be planned. |
| 2. Python discovery, platforms and environment | major | Allow dash to use whichever Python Stata finds or the user sets with Stata's own commands (set python_exec, python set exec). | Python discovery (plat:PT-1): fix, so that dash and dash_setup never refuse a Python that Stata itself accepts and never insist on their own discovery. The other items (platforms, editions, version floor, lock coverage, ~ on Windows, large-data performance, WebGL) have no ruling in D40 and stay open. |
| 3. Stata graph integration and output medium | major | Note this in the help file; that resolves the package. | Resolved by documentation. The help must state that a dashboard is not a Stata graph. name() registers a graph, there is no graph in memory, and graph export/save/use/display/combine, putdocx/putpdf/putexcel, dyndoc and markdown cannot use it. It must also cover each listed rc and message difference (name() length and '__', a duplicate name, saving() onto an existing file, nodraw with saving(, asis), play(), name(g, rep), dash clear g2, browser previews and leftover s() macros), and build-time evaluation of the data. |
| 4. Plot types and command grammar | major | heatmap is not a Stata command and sunflower is not a twoway plot type (see the notes above). | The heatmap and sunflower items are withdrawn. Audit correction made with this ruling: the internal and undocumented class names (rbarm, rbspike, ksm, patch, normgen, ipoint, meta_*, y*xview) and adopath-defined types are withdrawn too. They came from the same source, Stata's ado file names, not from documented twoway plot types. Help follow-on: dash.sthlp:737-742 must stop calling sunflower a twoway plot type, and dash's refusal of sunflower should be the same as for any word that is not a twoway plot type. The remaining items (matamatrix(), capitalised type names, the empty graph's axes and options, a bare plot after a parenthesised group, || inside one group) have no separate ruling. They are carried forward under the package-11 ruling (compatible with twoway) unless the maintainer says otherwise. |
| 5. Resource limits | major | For all caps on the number of observations, make sure they are documented in the help file. | Observation caps (the embedded-cell cap of 5M with its 20M ceiling, its counting of _N whatever if/in selects, and by()'s group limit as it bears on observations) are to be documented in the help: in Differences from twoway and in Resource limits. Caps that are not on observations (plots per graph, reflines, added texts, n(), bin(), lpoly degree, the 1,000-bin rebinning, the 2,000-tick clamp, msize, the fpfit grid, text length) have no ruling in D40 and stay open. |
| 6. by() fidelity | major | Resolve if supported by Bokeh. | Fix every item Bokeh can draw. Items Bokeh cannot draw have no ruling yet; the proposal for planning is to document them under Differences. |
| 7. Colour variables (colorvar) | major | Resolve if supported by Bokeh. | As package 6. |
| 8. Legend, titles and added text | major | Resolve if supported by Bokeh. | As package 6. |
| 9. Axes | major | Resolve if supported by Bokeh. | As package 6. |
| 10. Schemes, styles and figure geometry | major | Resolve if supported by Bokeh. | As package 6. |
| 11. Accepted syntax and refusals | major | Make dash compatible with twoway on these issues. | Fix all: accept what twoway accepts, refuse what twoway refuses, with twoway's rc and message. |
| 12. Statistical and computed layers | major | If there are zero observations after the if/in qualifiers, issue an error before the graph is created in Python, for every plot type, through a single function. If that differs from Stata, note it in the help; that note resolves it. | Zero observations (DG-1, the contour crash, and every other type): one Stata-side check, run after each plot's and the graph's if/in are applied and before Python is called, refuses with an error. Where twoway draws something instead (for example an empty graph), the help documents the difference, and that is the resolution. The other items (rarea cmissing outlines, the contour subsample above 1,000 points, a contour with no surface, xlog/xtransform() on lfit, lpolyci at zero df, documented numeric limits, tie order) have no ruling in D40 and stay open. |
| 13. Verification and test coverage | major | Top priority. Produce it so that it can be injected into the README to demonstrate the command's flexibility. | Plan first. A broad twoway-vs-dash corpus comparison, for example every example in Stata's own twoway help files and g.pdf, run under both twoway and dash. Its output (command, twoway picture, dash picture, verdict) is generated in a form that can be injected into README.md as a gallery. |
| 14. recast() and horizontal | minor | Resolve. | Fix all. |
| 15. Plot-option fidelity: markers, labels, lines, fills | minor | Resolve. | Fix all. |
| 16. Help, README and tracker accuracy | minor | Resolve. | Fix all. |
| 17. Messages and notes | cosmetic | Resolve. | Fix all. |

Planning order: 13 (Verification and test coverage), 1 (Default drawing of common graphs), 11 (Accepted syntax and refusals), 2 (Python discovery, platforms and environment), 12 (Statistical and computed layers), 3 (Stata graph integration and output medium), 4 (Plot types and command grammar), 5 (Resource limits), 6 (by() fidelity), 7 (Colour variables (colorvar)), 8 (Legend, titles and added text), 9 (Axes), 10 (Schemes, styles and figure geometry), 14 (recast() and horizontal), 15 (Plot-option fidelity: markers, labels, lines, fills), 16 (Help, README and tracker accuracy), 17 (Messages and notes).

## Verdict

No. The claim is not true today (before the D40 rulings are carried out). dash parses most twoway syntax and draws most twoway graphs closely, but it falls short in six ways. (1) The first drawing differs from twoway on very common commands: every default histogram's bin axis, the x ticks of every by() graph, the shared y title of every by() graph (never painted), yscale(range(0)), every default area plot (no outline), aspectratio() with xsize()/ysize(), ci layers with nofit, date axes whose end rows have a missing y, and a(b)c label rules that cross zero. (2) It refuses valid twoway: matamatrix(), repeated label options, p3()-p19(), a bare plot after a parenthesised group, values outside dash's own bounds, data over 20M cells, an unknown scheme(), and play(). It also accepts things twoway refuses. (3) name() registers a graph instead of drawing it, there is no Stata graph in memory, and a do-file cannot export an image, so twoway-then-graph-export do-files break. (4) It needs Python and bokeh, and it refuses a Python that Stata would find by itself. It has run only on macOS arm64 console StataNow 19.5 MP. (5) About 50 twoway options and many sub-options are accepted with a note but not drawn. (6) Several help and README sentences are false. The audit areas produced about 400 findings; none were refuted, and they are deduplicated into the items below. Most are untracked: BUGS.md has one open entry (natscale), and its build-time claim is wrong. Ids carry area prefixes where they collide: plot: (plot types), basic: (plain type options), stat: (statistical types), plat: (platform), syn: (syntax-data), sil: (silent-divergence).

## Work packages

### 1. Default drawing of common graphs (blocker, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** No ruling in D40.  
**For planning:** Open. The standing rule (draw what twoway draws) applies; to be planned.

Each item alone was graded major. Together they change the first drawing of the most common twoway commands (every default histogram and by() graph, every area plot, ysc(r(0)), aspectratio with xsize), so 'draws what twoway draws' fails for ordinary do-files.

- **The histogram bin axis is ticked over the bar edges; twoway ticks it over the first and last bin midpoints** [AX-4, VF-1, DG-2; confirmed; tracked: untracked (test exclusion only)]
  - Evidence: default_axes_truth P08 (scale 13.8125-39.1875, ticks 15(5)40); dash draws 10(10)40 with an empty band, and rep78 discrete 0(2)6 against twoway's 1(1)5; histogram is in _DASH_STATA_TICK_SKIP (dash_render.py:41811); test_default_axes.py:230-233 excludes P08 x
  - Fix: Use the midpoints as the tick input and keep the edges in the hull, at build, in the served refit and in the saved-page refit (or ask Stata over the midpoints); remove the exclusion; add discrete, bin(), width(), frequency and horizontal rows
- **by() panels tick x1 at the scheme's #5; twoway's by() styles set xlabels(#4) ylabels(#4)** [AX-2, TR-3, BY-26; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: bygraphstyle-default/compact/altleg/econ.style:39-40; _dash_by_numticks (dash_render.py:40785-40801) lowers y only; by_scales_truth d40 records #4 on x; probes on length, price, displacement, lfit range(), histogram width(5)
  - Fix: Use nx-1 on x1 (x2/y2 keep the scheme count, f02/c17); request n=4 from Stata's tick table; add truth rows whose x has #4 different from #5; correct dash.sthlp:4679
- **xscale/yscale(range()) beyond the data widens the default ticks; twoway ticks only the data** [AX-1; confirmed; tracked: untracked (misattributed residual in closed BUGS P1.17b)]
  - Evidence: Probes R1-R3 and C63; axis_truth b2_range_0/_100/_three draw 10..40; dash_render.py:47748-47768; exclusion test_bar_range_geometry.py:653-656; dash.sthlp:4529 says range() widens the ticks
  - Fix: On a default numeric axis, tick over the data and keep range() in the hull only (build, served, saved refit); keep widening for #N and by(); pin R1-R3
- **A by() graph's shared y title is never painted (its cell canvas is 1 px tall)** [VF-2, TR-24; confirmed (VF-2); TR-24 was uncertain and is settled by VF-2; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: Title visible=true, bbox 28x560, canvas 28x1 with 0 inked px; _dash_by_text_cell frame_height=1 (dash_render.py:55040) is never cleared in _dash_by_cell (55398-55405); the browser tests check bboxes and visibility only
  - Fix: Clear frame_height when the cell fills its track; check the dash_by_legend/clegend cells; add a pixel-level browser test
- **aspectratio() with xsize()/ysize(): the fixed frame is ignored by BokehJS (aspectratio(2) xsize(8) ysize(4) draws a 0 px plot), and a Bokeh E-1023 ERROR line is printed** [VF-3, ST-12; confirmed; tracked: untracked (BUGS WP-31 D5 marked fixed)]
  - Evidence: _dash_frame_sizes (dash_render.py:27654-27678) sets frame sizes but not width/height; q04 draws 600x560 with a 544x509 frame; min/max_height 160/560 against the default height 600; dash.sthlp:5670-5679; test_sizing.py:262-275 checks the model only
  - Fix: Set width/height (or None) and clear min/max_height on fixed figures; add a headless test of frame size and ratio; assert no Bokeh validation output
- **A default area plot (and its legend key) has no outline in any scheme** [PIC-1, RH-14; confirmed; tracked: untracked (GAPS3 E8 fixed rarea/rbar only)]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:18711-18733 strokes only when lcolor/lwidth/lpattern/lstyle is given; twoway strokes p#arealine at 2x parea, clipped, in all 10 schemes run; test_scheme_linewidth.py:279 injects lcolor(red)
  - Fix: Always draw the outline from the p#area line chain, inner-aligned, on the key too, keeping cmissing breaks
- **A ci type with nofit still uses two plot numbers, so every later layer takes the wrong p# style** [PIC-3; confirmed (legend-number knock-on unverified); tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twoway_lfitci_parse.class sets .n=1 under nofit; _dash_plot_style_indices (dash_render.py:57783-57787) always adds 2; gr_example #78 scatter drawn p5 where twoway draws p3
  - Fix: Count one plot under nofit and check fitplot()/ciplot(); add nofit rows to ci_plot_index_truth; check legend numbering
- **A date axis loses Stata's ticks when the layer's first or last row has a missing y (every L./D. operator, any series with a missing end)** [TR-4; partly (measurement confirmed; the cause is the missing-end rows, not the operator columns); tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: area/line d.gnp96 date draws 1967q2..2002q2 in 35-quarter steps against twoway's 1970q1(40)2000q1; the plain-y probe V1 is wrong too; x is summarised over rows with a missing y, so _dash_stata_pick (dash_render.py:41708) finds no match
  - Fix: Summarise only rows where all of the layer's variables are non-missing (twoway's omit rule); add operator and plain-missing rows to ts_operator_truth
- **a(b)c label and tick rules that cross zero print floating-point residue ('5.551e-17') as the zero label** [DG-3; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: _dash_expand_numlist (dash_render.py:40061-40082) computes a+b*k in plain floats; ylabel(-.3(.1).3) and -.7(.1).7
  - Fix: Reproduce Stata numlist's rounding (snap to 0, round relative to b); add a truth probe
- **Axes drawn at build from dash's natscale model, not Stata's: the by() count 4, mtick/mlabel #N, fit/kdensity/function/immediate ranges, the qfitci||scatter overlay, and the colour key's levels** [AX-3, TR-5, TR-12, DT-27, VF-10, CV-13; confirmed (TR-5 cause unproven; VF-10 partly: the computed axes matched in the probes and truth rows); tracked: BUGS.md open natscale entry (scope understated); computed axes partly documented at dash.sthlp:4529]
  - Evidence: Stata is asked only for counts {5} plus #N matched by the label/tick regex (dash_render.py:41832-41838); skip set at 41811; build-time misses B2, C42, B6, B4, E2; qfitci||scatter draws y 0(10)40 where twoway draws 10(10)40; colour-key tick calls pass no table (16773, 29219, 52420): zlabel(#3) gives 3 colours where twoway gives 2; BUGS.md:4139-4143 and ROADMAP.md:5153 say the drawing is 'exact at build'
  - Fix: At export, ask _natscale/_tsnatscale for the by() counts, mtick/mlabel #N, every computed lo/hi and the colour variable's range; correct the BUGS/ROADMAP scope

### 2. Python discovery, platforms and environment (major, effort XL)

**Decision (D40):** Allow dash to use whichever Python Stata finds or the user sets with Stata's own commands (set python_exec, python set exec).  
**For planning:** Python discovery (plat:PT-1): fix, so that dash and dash_setup never refuse a Python that Stata itself accepts and never insist on their own discovery. The other items (platforms, editions, version floor, lock coverage, ~ on Windows, large-data performance, WebGL) have no ruling in D40 and stay open.

twoway runs everywhere with no dependencies. dash refuses to run on a machine where Stata found Python by itself, and it is unverified off one platform and one edition.

- **dash and dash_setup refuse a Python that Stata found by itself (python_exec never set), and the remedy loops back to the same refusal** [plat:PT-1; confirmed (whether GUI Stata saves the Python it found is unmeasured); tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: fresh-HOME probe: c(python_exec) is empty while python query gives r(execpath)=/usr/local/bin/python3 and initialized=0; dash.ado:290-319; dash_setup.ado:96-107; dash_setup_lib.py:829-834
  - Fix: Fall back to quietly python query r(execpath), which does not start Python, in _dash_require_python, dash_setup and _dash_child_python; fix the message; add a cert test with a fresh HOME
- **Windows, Linux, Intel macOS and GUI Stata have never run dash; the opener and os.name branches are simulated only; the runners are POSIX sh** [plat:PT-5, TR-20, ECO-17; confirmed; tracked: ROADMAP 'Deferred: the platform package (P1.34.6)'; GAPS3 I1 has no Tracked line]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:6701-6707, 2521, 2722; dash.ado:10115-10200; dash_render.py:3598-3764; test_serve_lifecycle skipif win32; scripts/stata_batch.sh:19
  - Fix: Port the runners (Windows /e); run cert, parity and browser tests on each platform and in the GUI; open a BUGS entry and a GAPS3 I1 Tracked line
- **Stata/BE, Stata/SE and Stata 19.0 are admitted but never run; every truth fixture comes from StataNow 19.5 MP; BE cannot raise maxvar for dash's working columns (r(900))** [plat:PT-3; confirmed; tracked: ROADMAP platform package]
  - Evidence: dash.pkg; dash.sthlp:6701-6703; limits.sthlp:25, 52 and note 2; _dash_maxvar_room (dash.ado:7856-7879)
  - Fix: Run make cert and parity on BE, SE and 19.0; re-measure the truth that depends on edition or update
- **Named graphs with function layers are capped by c(macrolen); BE's fixed 264,392 refuses two n(10000) layers, and the advice 'set maxvar' is impossible on BE** [plat:PT-4; confirmed; tracked: n() refusal documented; the BE part is untracked]
  - Evidence: probe3 r(134) at 408,235 characters; dash.ado:11366-11377; dash.sthlp:3713-3714
  - Fix: Keep the points in a strL scalar or a file, as the one-line route does; fix the message and the help
- **The version floor is Stata 19 (r(9)); twoway runs on Stata 8 and later** [plat:PT-2; partly (a documented package requirement); tracked: ROADMAP platform package; maintainer decision M2]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:84-89, 438-447; dash.sthlp:6701-6707; README.md:32-33
  - Fix: Certify 16-18.5 (Break fallback, rpcap/rpspike advice), or scope the claim to Stata 19+
- **Python 3.11+ with bokeh 3.8-3.9, numpy and pandas is required; no conda guidance; dash_setup reports an old numpy/pandas as unmet and does not upgrade it** [plat:PT-11, ECO-17; partly (dash itself only notes an old numpy/pandas); tracked: dash.sthlp Dependencies]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:351-400; dash_setup.ado:439-507; dash.sthlp:6709-6719
  - Fix: State the requirement next to the claim and under Differences; document conda; consider a dash_setup venv() default
- **The hash-pinned lock covers Python 3.11-3.14 on five platform tags: Python 3.15 and Windows ARM64 (tzdata) fail, and there is no offline wheelhouse route** [plat:PT-12; confirmed; tracked: generic in dash_setup.sthlp; specifics untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_requirements.txt:6-7, 11, 83, 600; dash_setup_lib.py:54, 430-437; no Python ceiling
  - Fix: Regenerate the lock per Python release, or warn above the newest locked Python; add win_arm64; document pip download plus PIP_NO_INDEX/PIP_FIND_LINKS
- **saving() with ~ on Windows: dash expands only '~/' from HOME and treats '~\' as relative** [plat:PT-6; confirmed from code and the manual (not run on Windows); tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:756-763; dash_parse.mata:1455-1475; [U] 11.6.2
  - Fix: Expand ~ as Stata does on each OS (USERPROFILE, both ~\ and ~/); add a cert case
- **'Works offline in any modern browser' is verified on Chrome only (Firefox once by hand, Safari never); bokeh 3.10+ unverified** [plat:PT-16; confirmed; tracked: ROADMAP S04, S41]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:172-174; BUGS P1.29 residual; dash_render.py:237
  - Fix: Run the CSP, browser and a11y tests in Firefox, Safari and Edge; verify each bokeh minor
- **Large data: about 6-8 s and 1.4 GB of Stata RSS per million rows; page columns are JSON number lists, about twice the base64 size** [plat:PT-14; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: probe1/probe2 timings and RSS; q1.html CDS entries; dash_render.py:2279
  - Fix: Serialise numeric columns as numpy arrays (base64); measure browser load time and memory at 1M rows and at the ceiling
- **WebGL above 10,000 dataset rows: thick dashed lines draw speckled; the switch counts the dataset's rows, not the graph's** [DG-14; partly (unverified on GPU WebGL); tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:5567, 63396, 70285; px_L02.png (SwiftShader only)
  - Fix: Keep dashed lines on the canvas backend; base the threshold on the drawn rows; add a WebGL-vs-canvas pixel check

### 3. Stata graph integration and output medium (major, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** Note this in the help file; that resolves the package.  
**For planning:** Resolved by documentation. The help must state that a dashboard is not a Stata graph. name() registers a graph, there is no graph in memory, and graph export/save/use/display/combine, putdocx/putpdf/putexcel, dyndoc and markdown cannot use it. It must also cover each listed rc and message difference (name() length and '__', a duplicate name, saving() onto an existing file, nodraw with saving(, asis), play(), name(g, rep), dash clear g2, browser previews and leftover s() macros), and build-time evaluation of the data.

A do-file that names its graphs, exports them, or combines them does not work unchanged under dash.

- **name() registers the graph instead of drawing it, and saving() beside name() writes nothing; a do-file that names every graph produces no output** [syn:SD-17, ECO-3; confirmed; tracked: dash.sthlp Differences item 2 (deliberate)]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:815-823 (Difference 2); probe p3 B1/B2
  - Fix: Either draw and register on name() and honour saving(), or state this beside the claim (README and synopsis)
- **A dashboard is not a Stata graph: graph export/save/display/combine, putdocx, putpdf, putexcel, dyndoc and markdown cannot use it; graph export writes a stale graph or r(601)** [GW-12, ECO-2, plat:PT-13, TR-16; confirmed (TR-16 partly: its 'dash graph' bullet is wrong); tracked: ROADMAP deferred P1.28 B3 / M8]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:172-182, 1631-1663; dash.ado:244; probes p1 F1/F4 and p2 A1/A2
  - Fix: Implement 'dash export using f.png|pdf|svg' (headless browser), or run the same twoway with nodraw under name() so graph memory works; state the limit next to the claim
- **A registered graph draws the data in memory at dash build, not at registration: preserve/collapse + name() + restore draws the restored data with no note; tempvar and frame graphs fail with r(111)** [ECO-1; partly; tracked: build-time evaluation documented at dash.sthlp:194-205; the rest untracked]
  - Evidence: p3 C/D: CDS shape [5] against [69]; p2 D4; dash.ado has no datasignature/c(frame) check
  - Fix: Snapshot the columns at registration, or fingerprint datasignature/c(N)/c(frame) and refuse or note at build; document that name() keeps no data
- **graph combine has no full counterpart: dash build refuses xcommon, ycommon, cols(), holes(), colfirst, iscale(), imargin(), note(), caption() and xsize(), and the help never maps combine to layout()** [ECO-4; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: probe p3 E; p1 F6
  - Fix: Document the mapping; consider shared ranges, note()/caption() and holes() on dash build
- **name(): at most 22 characters and no '__'; a duplicate name gives r(198) in dash's words where twoway gives r(110) 'graph X already exists'** [GW-13, ECO-6, ECO-12; confirmed; tracked: dash.sthlp 'What a name may be' (the rc is not listed)]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:11294-11303, 11360-11364; dash.sthlp:861-897; test_registry_collision.cscript:987
  - Fix: Exit 110 with twoway's line; key the registry so 32-character names and '__' fit
- **saving() onto an existing file gives r(198) where twoway gives r(602); twoway's '(file x.gph not found)' note is missing** [GW-14, ECO-13; confirmed; tracked: dash.sthlp Differences item 2]
  - Evidence: probe p1 I1/I2; dash.sthlp:825-829; dash.ado:11458-11508
  - Fix: Exit 602 with twoway's words
- **nodraw with saving(, asis) writes the page where twoway gives r(601)** [GW-15; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: probe v1; dash.sthlp:848-852; ND-06
  - Fix: Refuse it, or keep it documented
- **play() refuses a readable recording (r(198)); there is no Graph Editor** [GW-16, ECO-14; confirmed; tracked: dash.sthlp Unsupported, play()]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:8239-8272; dash.sthlp:6667-6683; U54
  - Fix: Keep documented (XL to interpret .grec)
- **name(g, rep) or another bad sub-option gives a garbled 'invalid graph name g??rep' where twoway says 'option rep not allowed'** [GW-9; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:11273-11303; probe p4
  - Fix: Parse name()'s sub-options as twoway does
- **'dash clear g2' clears the whole registry (no syntax check); there is no dir/describe/drop for registered items** [ECO-5; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:11948-11997; probe p1 J1
  - Fix: Refuse arguments, or drop only the names given; consider registry commands
- **Each one-line command opens a new browser page; only the newest five previews are kept; saving() opens none** [ECO-15; partly; tracked: mostly documented]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:10045-10200; dash.sthlp:1608-1611, 1769, 2864-2867
  - Fix: Document one page per command, or reuse one preview per session
- **dash leaves internal s() macros (s(_recast_type), s(g_saving), s(g_name)); twoway clears s()** [ECO-7; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:154-185; probes p1/p2
  - Fix: sreturn clear at the end of each route

### 4. Plot types and command grammar (major, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** heatmap is not a Stata command and sunflower is not a twoway plot type (see the notes above).  
**For planning:** The heatmap and sunflower items are withdrawn. Audit correction made with this ruling: the internal and undocumented class names (rbarm, rbspike, ksm, patch, normgen, ipoint, meta_*, y*xview) and adopath-defined types are withdrawn too. They came from the same source, Stata's ado file names, not from documented twoway plot types. Help follow-on: dash.sthlp:737-742 must stop calling sunflower a twoway plot type, and dash's refusal of sunflower should be the same as for any word that is not a twoway plot type. The remaining items (matamatrix(), capitalised type names, the empty graph's axes and options, a bare plot after a parenthesised group, || inside one group) have no separate ruling. They are carried forward under the package-11 ruling (compatible with twoway) unless the maintainer says otherwise.

A documented [G-2] plot type is refused with a false message, and some valid command shapes are refused.

- **matamatrix() plots are refused by name; on non-yxview types, where twoway gives r(111), dash gives r(198)** [plot:PT-10; confirmed; tracked: dash.sthlp Not drawn; BUGS B3]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:5935-5940; dash.sthlp:737-743; twoway_yxview_parse.class:151-180
  - Fix: Read the matrix columns into export columns for the yxview types
- **Plot-type names are read in any case (SC, Hist, Contour) where twoway refuses capitalised abbreviations** [plot:PT-3, basic:PT-14, DT-14; confirmed; tracked: dash.sthlp 'Case']
  - Evidence: dash.ado:5869; dash_parse.mata:2526-2528; dash.sthlp:528-532
  - Fix: Match abbreviations in lower case only; decide on capitalised full names (file-system dependent; Linux unverified)
- **The empty graph is drawn with two black axes and takes axis options twoway refuses ('xlabels() not allowed, xaxis1 does not exist')** [plot:PT-4, syn:SD-2; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: empty_tw.svg has no axes; dash_render.py:63570-63576; test_bare_dash.py:74-80; p3out E20-E51; dash.sthlp:164-166
  - Fix: Draw no axes when there is no layer; refuse axis, scale and line options there in twoway's words (plural names); add an SVG truth for twoway, title(T)
- **A bare plot after a parenthesised group, '(scatter ..) line ..', is refused; twoway draws it** [syn:SD-1; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_parse.mata:3622-3640 (the comment at 3627-3630 is false); p2/p11 logs; P1B-37 pins dash's message
  - Fix: Follow twoway's _parse expand: text up to the next || is another plot; give twoway's '<word> is not a twoway plot type' and r(111) cases
- **A || inside one group with no graph options, '(scatter .. || line ..)', is refused; twoway draws both** [syn:SD-13; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: p2out S13; probe v1
  - Fix: Accept the option-less form, or document it

  Withdrawn under D40: plot:PT-1, stat:PT-17, DT-01, TR-1; plot:PT-9; plot:PT-2, RH-16, plot:PT-12.

### 5. Resource limits (major, effort M)

**Decision (D40):** For all caps on the number of observations, make sure they are documented in the help file.  
**For planning:** Observation caps (the embedded-cell cap of 5M with its 20M ceiling, its counting of _N whatever if/in selects, and by()'s group limit as it bears on observations) are to be documented in the help: in Differences from twoway and in Resource limits. Caps that are not on observations (plots per graph, reflines, added texts, n(), bin(), lpoly degree, the 1,000-bin rebinning, the 2,000-tick clamp, msize, the fpfit grid, text length) have no ruling in D40 and stay open.

twoway has no caps. Large datasets and many-plot or many-panel graphs are refused or truncated, and these limits are not under Differences.

- **r(134) above 5M embedded cells (hard ceiling 20M, about 10M obs for one y and one x), 100 plots, 10,000 reflines and 1,000 added texts** [plat:PT-8, syn:SD-16, sil:SD-22; confirmed; tracked: dash.sthlp Resource limits (not in Differences)]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:2360-2375; dash.sthlp:2933-3000; probes: 11M obs refused, 300 plots refused
  - Fix: Draw large data (aggregate or decimate with a note) or raise the defaults; list every limit under Differences
- **The cell cap counts _N whatever if/in select (the qualifier adds a column), so 'in 1/100' on 3M obs is refused although the page embeds only the selected rows** [plat:PT-7, DG-15; confirmed (plat:PT-7 partly: deliberate, documented); tracked: documented at dash.sthlp:2950-2953, 3048-3050]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:5222-5254, 11720-11727, 13170-13176; probe1/probe2
  - Fix: Count the selected rows when no filter, link() or csv needs every row, and export only those
- **The plot limit is per graph, not twoway's 100 variables per plot: dash takes y1-y100 x (twoway r(103)) and refuses (y1-y60 x)(y61-y120 x), which twoway draws** [plat:PT-9; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: limits.sthlp:104; probe6/probe7; dash.ado:5204-5219
  - Fix: Refuse more than 100 variables in one plot with r(103) 'too many variables specified'; raise or relax the per-graph cap
- **by() draws the first 100 groups (ceiling 1,000), with a note** [plat:PT-10, BY-22; confirmed; tracked: Resource limits]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:2366; dash.sthlp:2976-2980, 3300-3304
  - Fix: Raise the default or list it under Differences
- **n() above 10,000 and bin() above 1,000 are refused; lpoly degree() above 30 is fitted at a lower degree** [stat:PT-15, DT-17; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: probe1 #3, #85, #177; dash.sthlp:1149-1172, 3711-3716
  - Fix: Lift n() on lpoly (capped at _N anyway); cap internally with a note instead of refusing
- **histogram width() or discrete needing more than 1,000 bins (empty ones counted) is rebinned: width() is dropped, and discrete is squeezed into 1,000 bars** [sil:SD-1; confirmed; tracked: untracked (TWOWAY_COVERAGE_AUDIT.md:98 mentions the cap)]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:22508, 22539-22576; twoway__histogram_gen.ado:205-216 tabulates occupied bins; price discrete/width(1)/width(10)
  - Fix: Bin only the occupied cells (at most N) in Python and JS; add a truth test
- **An axis option asking for more than 2,000 ticks keeps the default ticks; #N above 2,000 is clamped silently** [AX-16, sil:SD-10; confirmed; tracked: partly in the Axis labels paragraph]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:40233, 42205, 42228-42233; twoway draws 2,501 labels
  - Fix: Document it under Resource limits, or at least widen the axis to the listed range
- **msize() above 1,000 units is drawn at the default with 'not a recognised size'; the help says any number** [sil:SD-11; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:8314, 8421-8424; dash.sthlp:1177-1178
  - Fix: Correct the help and the note wording, or raise the bound
- **fpfit/fpfitci above 20,000 distinct x is evaluated on a linspace grid, silently** [sil:SD-12; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:429, 21747, 27031
  - Fix: Name the cap in a note or the help
- **text() whose globals expand past 4,000 characters is refused r(198)** [sil:SD-13; partly; tracked: documented; BUGS D13]
  - Evidence: dash_parse.mata:11461-11469; dash.sthlp:784-785, 5232-5234
  - Fix: None needed (documented), or raise to c(macrolen)

### 6. by() fidelity (major, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** Resolve if supported by Bokeh.  
**For planning:** Fix every item Bokeh can draw. Items Bokeh cannot draw have no ruling yet; the proposal for planning is to document them under Differences.

by() is one of the most used twoway options. Several of its sub-options and interactions draw or refuse differently.

- **contour/contourline under by() cuts each panel at its own z ticks; twoway uses one shared z axis, so the same colour means a different z per panel** [BY-28; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: zyx2view_g.class:255-276; tw_f29a.svg has 3 colours in both panels; dash dump shows per-panel cuts; _dash_share_by_scales shares x/y only
  - Fix: Compute the cuts once over all panels (Total included), in both modes; measure the exact cuts from a drawn graph
- **by()'s note(), caption() and subtitle() with ring(0) are dropped silently ('Graphs by' vanishes too)** [BY-1; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: _dash_by_chrome sides=('above',)/('below',) (dash_render.py:55079, 55082); skipped at 54495
  - Fix: Draw them inside the panel array at their clock, or at least name them; add truth probes
- **A scale() written outside by() scales every panel, where twoway ignores it on a by() graph; by(, scale()), which twoway applies, is named and ignored** [BY-2, BY-29, BY-11; confirmed; tracked: untracked (by(scale()) only under the generic sentence dash.sthlp:3363-3364)]
  - Evidence: twoway SVGs identical with and without scale(1.5) or scale(0.5); _dash_scale_factor (dash_render.py:27487) has no by() check; by(scale()) falls to the unsupported branch (36404)
  - Fix: Ignore the outer scale() under by(); apply by(, scale()) to the whole by() graph
- **function range(varname) under by() spans the whole data in every panel; twoway uses each panel's own range (kdensity range(varname) likely the same, unprobed)** [BY-27; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twoway__function_gen.ado:101-102; _dash_fold_range (dash.ado:2401-2432); dash.ado:8845-8873
  - Fix: Compute the min/max per panel and for Total inside the per-panel loop; pin it against sersets
- **An at() hole for a legend or colour key that is not drawn is not reserved in the grid** [BY-3; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: bygraph_g.class:612-618, 670-676; dash_render.py:55786-55874, 55990-56024
  - Fix: Return the at() cell whenever at() is given and the key object exists
- **compact: beside an explicit style() it is drawn compact (twoway lets style() win), and its black plot-region and label-box outlines are not drawn** [BY-4, sil:SD-2; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: bygraph_g.class:291-293; bygraphstyle-compact.style:39 special_options; _DASH_BYSTYLE_EXPAND has no compact entry; by_remainder_truth bystyle_compact is unused
  - Fix: Apply compact only without style(); expand compact's special options
- **aspectratio() with by(, shrink) is accepted; twoway refuses it ('options aspect() and shrink may not be combined')** [BY-5; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: bygraph_g.class:559-566; probe1 T14
  - Fix: Refuse it in twoway's words
- **by(, total) with no variables gives r(198) in dash's words; twoway gives r(100) 'varlist required in by()'** [BY-6; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:4263-4267; probe1 T17
  - Fix: Use twoway's line and r(100)
- **by(, l1title() b1title() t1title() ...) are named and ignored; in twoway l1title/b1title replace the shared axis titles** [BY-12; confirmed; tracked: generic dash.sthlp:3363-3364]
  - Evidence: bygraph_g.class:203-216; probes T4-T7
  - Fix: Draw the eight edge titles on the by() graph
- **by(, legloc() clegloc() plegend() plegloc()) are named and ignored** [BY-13; confirmed; tracked: generic]
  - Evidence: bygraph_g.class:142-144; P6 equals P6b; T13
  - Fix: Merge them into the legend/clegend placement; implement plegend()
- **coloruseplegend under by(): plegend(on) draws nothing, and clegend(on) draws a discrete Legend with '(missing)' where twoway draws a colour-bar key** [BY-14; partly (the dash key does carry the title); tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: probes T10-T12; kp.py
  - Fix: Implement by(, plegend()) and twoway's key form
- **Panel-label sub-options twoway draws are named: justification(), nobexpand, lwidth()/lpattern(), span, alignment(), linegap(), bmargin(), placement(), orientation()** [BY-10; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: probes T29, P1-P3, P13; dash.sthlp:5382-5385
  - Fix: Draw them on the panel label row
- **by() panel labels: a date variable is written as dates (twoway writes 14610); a value label holding double quotes stays one line** [BY-16, BY-17; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:3280-3287
  - Fix: Write the raw value as twoway does; split quoted parts
- **Documented by() layout differences: style(combine) on by()'s grid; clock 3/9 labels and titles below instead of beside; aspectratio() shapes each panel figure, not its plot region; a smaller graph margin insets nothing** [BY-18, BY-19, BY-20, BY-21; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:3330-3362, 3342-3343, 5372-5373, 5683-5684
  - Fix: Port combine's regions, beside-panel placement and per-frame aspect
- **by() panels in the first column have narrower plot regions (y labels inside their canvas), so pixels per unit differ across a row** [VF-6; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: q03 407 vs 448 px; twoway regions equal
  - Fix: Give every panel the same left border, or draw the y labels in an outer column
- **xoverhangs/yoverhangs move panel borders under by() (twoway ignores them there) and use every label, not only the end labels, raising both sides equally** [GW-5; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: advanced_options.sthlp:151-156; _dash_overhangs (dash_render.py:27704) called per panel at 64005; sizing_truth overhang_*
  - Fix: Skip under by(); measure only the end labels per side
- **A weighted layer under by() with a panel whose weights are all zero is drawn; twoway stops with r(198)** [sil:SD-7; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:6952; probes q1, p2
  - Fix: Apply the check per panel
- **A legend in a by() at() hole keeps full-size text; twoway scales it (0.85x)** [PIC-13; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: tw64 vs tw65; d65 label size 10pt
  - Fix: Scale the hole legend by by()'s size multiplier
- **by(, legend(pos(abc))) gives no note and the wrong side; by(, bgcolor()) is named although nothing visible changes under stcolor** [BY-7, BY-15; confirmed; tracked: untracked / generic]
  - Evidence: probe1 T20, T8
  - Fix: Print twoway's clockdir note with its fallback; take bgcolor in silence or draw it
- **Residuals in closed by()/span entries are filed nowhere: 'panel row taller than its panels (unfiled)', by(clegend(pos(6))) keeps panel proportions** [TR-19; confirmed; tracked: closed-entry residuals]
  - Evidence: BUGS.md:3999-4095
  - Fix: Open BUGS entries for them

### 7. Colour variables (colorvar) (major, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** Resolve if supported by Bokeh.  
**For planning:** As package 6.

The default colouring of every non-line colorvar plot and every discrete key differ from twoway.

- **An unbinned numeric colorvar() on 17 non-line types is a 256-step gradient with a ColorBar; twoway bins at the z ticks with a stepped key (mono schemes start near white)** [CV-8, DT-22, sil:SD-20; confirmed; tracked: dash.sthlp 'How the colours are drawn is dash's']
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:16730-16760; dash.sthlp:5523-5530; test_colorvar_truth.py:91-92 skips the default; K10/T7/I7
  - Fix: Route every type through the stepped mapper and draw the stepped key, as lines already are
- **colorfillonly strips the level colour from every line part (line, connected, spike, dropline, rcapsym, pc shafts and heads)** [CV-1; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:16983-16984, 17145-17147; F1-F8; colorvar_options.sthlp:178-185
  - Fix: Keep line_color mapped on line glyphs; keep the series colour only on marker and bar outlines
- **The discrete/binned clegend differs: ascending top to bottom, value labels and interval texts instead of a z axis, an extra '(missing)'/otherlabel entry, circles for every type, a frame** [CV-3; confirmed; tracked: partly documented (dash.sthlp:5536)]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:16319-16698; K1-K4, K8, T1-T5, L4; TestOtherlabel compares against the plegend probe
  - Fix: Draw twoway's axis-style key: highest at top, z axis at natscale ticks or cuts, no missing entry, the type's key swatch
- **The coloruseplegend key: ascending order, '(missing)' for 'Other', an extra title, value labels; legend() sub-options and colorkeysrange applied** [CV-4; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:16606, 16698; K5, K6, M4, Q3, C28
  - Fix: Descending levels plus 'Other', no title, display-format numbers; keep legend() off it
- **colordiscrete with colorlevels(k) makes k-1 cuts at range/k; twoway makes k cuts at range/(k+1)** [CV-2; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: yxview.class:2406-2427; dash_render.py:14227-14230; test_colorvar_forms.py:128 pins dash's rule; disc_levels_price_4 passes vacuously
  - Fix: Use k+1 steps under colordiscrete (add min/max with colorvarminmax), also in the relevel path
- **legend(size()) and legend(rows()/cols()) restyle the colour key; twoway's key ignores them** [CV-5; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:39703-39735; Q1, Q3, P12
  - Fix: Exclude colorvar keys from legend()'s grid and size steps
- **Two colorvar layers: dash draws a key per layer and no plot legend; twoway draws the last layer's key plus a plot legend** [CV-6; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:16859-16886; M1, K11
  - Fix: One key from the last colorvar layer; keep plot-legend items
- **A colorvar layer's plot-legend swatch takes row 0's level colour, not its pstyle colour** [CV-7; confirmed (inferred from the model, not a rendered pixel); tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: D_K9 field-mapped renderer; bokeh.js:32111-32112
  - Fix: Use a proxy swatch in the pstyle colour
- **colorkeysreverse reverses entries and labels (twoway reverses colours only); colorlevels() over one value prints nonsense intervals** [CV-11, CV-12; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:16598-16599, 14228; R1, I5
  - Fix: Reverse colours only; use zero-width cuts
- **colorvar refusals differ in rc or words: colorcuts(abc) r(198) vs r(121); colorvar(a b) r(198) vs r(101); colorvar(pri*) accepted vs r(198); colorlevels(abc) and nosuch in dash's words** [CV-9, syn:SD-9; confirmed (syn:SD-9 partly: the string colorvar is documented); tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:3699-3701, 4329; run1 A1/A2/A5, C5-C7; p3out F09/F13
  - Fix: Use twoway's rc and line; refuse wildcards
- **Malformed clegend(bogus), plegend(bogus), zlabel(bogus) and zscale(bogus) are drawn with a note (garbled 'using not drawn') where twoway refuses r(198)** [CV-10, LT-14; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:8776-8782; dash_render.py:16917-16918, 53261; run1 A8, run6 P1/P5/P6/P9
  - Fix: Run zlabel through _dash_axlabel_refusal and zscale through the scale check; refuse unknown clegend/plegend words
- **Documented acceptances: string colorvar, colpalette()/colornlevels()/colormissing(), cv()/colv(), an empty layer drawing no key, '(missing)' for all-missing** [CV-14; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:1258-1260, 5422, 5499-5500, 5537-5540, 5573-5576
  - Fix: Keep deliberate, or refuse as twoway does
- **On a discrete key, zlabel() and zscale() are named and not drawn; clegend() altaxis, region(), bplacement(), bmargin(), subtitle(), note() and caption() are named as 'not a recognised sub-option'** [CV-15, LT-23; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:5471-5478, 5596-5597; _DASH_CLEGEND_SUBOPTS (dash_render.py:53239-53241)
  - Fix: Draw them once the axis-style key exists; reword the note
- **clegend(title()) replaces the z title and prints the raw option text when it has sub-options ('"Z", box')** [LT-12; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:53271-53272, 16898-16899; C_ti, C_cbx
  - Fix: Parse it as a textbox and draw it above the key, keeping ztitle
- **The z tick family: zlabels/zticks/zmlabels/zmticks are refused (twoway takes them); ztick(), zmlabel() and zmtick() are noted and not drawn, and are absent from the Unsupported list** [AX-12, syn:SD-4; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twowaygraph_g.class:122-123; dash.ado:995, 1324; dash_parse.mata:8151-8156; Z01-Z32
  - Fix: Accept the plurals; draw the ticks and labels on the key, or document them
- **The colour bar is a fixed 12 px (twoway uses the scheme's clegend_width, about 23 px); the scheme contour keys (outline, colorlines, reversekey, levels) are never read** [sil:SD-4, sil:SD-14; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:16751, 24622; scheme-s2color.scheme:56, 185, 2239-2241
  - Fix: Read clegend_width/height and the four contour keys
- **contourline with colorlines draws no level legend; plain contourline gets a needless note; the contour key title sits above with no axis line** [LT-22; partly; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:592-593, 5527-5533; CL_ctl, CL_cl
  - Fix: Build the level legend under colorlines; drop the note on plain contourline

### 8. Legend, titles and added text (major, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** Resolve if supported by Bokeh.  
**For planning:** As package 6.

Legend and title defaults and sub-options visibly differ, including the default title style and placement.

- **legend(title()/subtitle()) are drawn at key-text size and colour, not the scheme's leg_title/leg_subtitle styles** [LT-1; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:39260-39304; stcolor 157.44 vs 112.56 px; s2color dknavy
  - Fix: Style them from textbox_style('leg_title'/'leg_subtitle'), with overrides
- **legend(ring(0) bplacement()) ignores bplacement; bplacement abbreviations (nor, nea, t, top) are not read** [LT-4, ST-1 (bplacement part); confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:39622-39627, 38491; L_r0bp, L_r0p1bp
  - Fix: Use bplacement for the inside location at ring(0); read compass2dir abbreviations
- **legend(nobox) removes the region box; in twoway nobox is the key-text textbox option and changes nothing** [LT-2; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:39305-39307; legend_geometry_truth nobox equals default; test_legend_geometry.py:386; dash.sthlp:4472
  - Fix: Treat box/nobox as the key-text box; fix the help and flip the test
- **Key-text textbox options are not drawn: box (silent), bcolor(), fcolor(), justification(), alignment(), margin(), linegap(), tstyle()** [LT-3; confirmed; tracked: generic dash.sthlp:1043-1049]
  - Evidence: _DASH_LEGEND_SUBOPTS (dash_render.py:53797-53803); probes L_box..L_ts
  - Fix: Draw them, or name box
- **legend(all), draw and nodraw are named: whether a legend appears, and how keys merge, differ** [LT-5; confirmed; tracked: generic]
  - Evidence: legend_g.class:161-240; L_mrgall, L_draw1, L_nodraw
  - Fix: Honour all (skip the merge), draw and nodraw
- **Legend layout sub-options named: style(), width(), height(), xoffset(), yoffset(), showindex, region(style()/lstyle()/lalign())** [LT-6; confirmed; tracked: generic; TWOWAY_COVERAGE_AUDIT.md:143]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:39145-39147, 53797; probes
  - Fix: Implement them on the Legend, or list them
- **bexpand on legend() and on title/subtitle/note/caption is taken silently where twoway widens the box** [LT-7; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:53792, 53832-53839; T_bex, M_bexp
  - Fix: Draw the expanded box, or name it
- **A title box under stcolor is filled #d9e6eb (the added-text fill), not textbox white with a 6.48 line** [LT-8; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:54211-54218; textboxstyle-heading.style; stcolor.scheme:43
  - Fix: Take the fill and line from the textbox style
- **A fractional ring() (7.5, 4.5) or ring(100) is dropped silently** [LT-9; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:54064, 39598; ringposstyle.sthlp
  - Fix: Parse ring as a real 0-100 and order by value
- **Numeric marginstyle forms (# # # #, l=#, l+#) are refused for textbox margins; legend region margin collapses four sides to the largest** [LT-10; confirmed; tracked: untracked (BUGS D11 fixed regions only)]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:10274-10292, 39150-39177
  - Fix: Route through _dash_margin_sides with per-side padding
- **text(..., alignment()) is noted and not drawn, though the help, CHANGELOG and WP-32 say it is drawn** [LT-11; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:9872-9876; _dash_at_canon (dash_parse.mata:11863-11904) drops it; dash.sthlp:767-770
  - Fix: Pass alignment through _dash_at_canon; add an end-to-end test
- **legend(t1title() ... b2title()) is accepted and named where twoway refuses r(198)** [LT-13; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:53560-53566; dash_parse.mata:9746-9751
  - Fix: Drop the eight edge titles from both legend tables
- **title(..., ring(x)) is accepted silently where twoway prints notes and refuses r(198)** [LT-14; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: _dash_textbox_position (dash_render.py:54059-54067); R_tring
  - Fix: Refuse a non-numeric title ring after twoway's notes
- **legend title()/subtitle() sub-options (position(), box ...) are dropped silently; subtitle size()/color() are ignored** [LT-15; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:39260-39304
  - Fix: Apply them, or name them
- **title/subtitle/note/caption placement() at ring(0) is drawn as a Label anchor (n lifts the box out of the plot) and long names, clocks and synonyms are ignored** [LT-16, ST-8; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:54187-54188, 54384-54388; T_r0pl; probe2 tw_1..19; dash.sthlp:5359-5361
  - Fix: Place the box at twoway's inside compass point; add a truth fixture
- **legend(holes()) with cols(1) inserts a full blank row** [LT-17; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: legend_g.class:312-357; dash_render.py:39497-39510
  - Fix: Mirror legend_g's hole placement
- **Title box sub-options named: orientation(), width(), height(), angle(), lstyle(), bl*, lalign(), style(); clocks 2-4/8-10 drawn above or below (style() and lalign() missing from the help list)** [LT-18, ST-18; confirmed; tracked: documented (partly)]
  - Evidence: _DASH_TEXTBOX_BOX_HONOURED (dash_render.py:53835-53839); dash.sthlp:5341-5345, 5379-5381
  - Fix: Draw them, or keep documented and add the two missing names
- **Edge titles draw no box, justification(), margin() or tstyle()** [LT-19; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:53886-53889; dash.sthlp:5316-5325
  - Fix: Reuse _dash_textbox_style
- **text() draws no width(), height(), linegap(), bexpand, bmargin(), lstyle(), lpattern(), lalign(); a `=exp' global is drawn literally** [LT-20, GW-21; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:9871-9881; dash.sthlp:773-783
  - Fix: Draw a fixed-size multi-line box and dashed frame
- **Legend keys: marker at 0.4 of the box, not the marker's size; textfirst, stacked, symplacement() and the legend's own note()/caption() not drawn; at 384 px an economist 3-column legend clips its labels (undocumented)** [LT-21, VF-8, PIC-14; confirmed; tracked: documented (D-31.a; ROADMAP M17), clipping untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:4453-4501; bokeh MarkerView 0.4; s_misc.png
  - Fix: Custom legend renderer; at minimum stop the clipping
- **Legend key shapes of rcap, rcapsym, pccapsym, dropline, pcarrow, pcbarrow and rarea are wrong** [PIC-11; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: z_key_zoom.png, y_rk_stcolor.png, p101_key.png
  - Fix: Legend-only renderers in twoway's key shapes
- **Keys are merged when two different pstyles look alike (s1mono line v6/v14, v12/v15)** [MS-10; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:39385-39431; A08, E21
  - Fix: Merge only by the same resolved p# with no style options
- **Partial SMCL markup ({bf:}, {it:}, {sup:} on letters, fonts) in titles, labels and keys is drawn in one plain style** [ST-14, TR-14, DG-17, PIC-18; confirmed; tracked: documented limit]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:5170-5183; BUGS.md:3689; graph_text.sthlp examples
  - Fix: Draw texts as positioned runs or HTML text
- **title("'90s") is drawn where twoway gives r(100)** [LT-24; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:1080-1091
  - Fix: None by design (or emulate)
- **A horizontal kdensity (and function) is keyed by its name; twoway keys it by the grid variable 'x'** [RH-13; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twoway_kdensity_parse.class:177-181; tw_Q25.svg; V5
  - Fix: Key it by the grid name when yxswitch applies

### 9. Axes (major, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** Resolve if supported by Bokeh.  
**For planning:** As package 6.

Axis sub-options and edge rules differ visibly; ytitle(, orientation(horizontal)) is a common idiom.

- **Axis title textbox sub-options other than size/color are named: orientation() (Bokeh axis_label_orientation never set), justification(), placement(), box, margin() ...** [AX-13, ST-13; confirmed; tracked: documented (generic)]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:53826-53827, 53961-53995; dash.sthlp:1099-1105
  - Fix: Draw orientation(), margins and justification; name the rest
- **custom on xlabel()/ylabel() is ignored: its styles reach every label and the grid; custom with no list is accepted (twoway r(198))** [AX-5; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: _gs_parse_and_log_tickset.ado:427-438; C01, C02, G4; dash.sthlp:4567-4568
  - Fix: Draw custom ticks as their own styled overlay; refuse the empty form
- **Gridlines of xtick()/xmtick() are drawn in the minor style (dotted); twoway uses the major gridstyle** [AX-6; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: scheme-s2color.scheme:1846; dash_render.py:44966-44972; C50, G1, G2; dash.sthlp:4867
  - Fix: Use the tickset's gridstyle
- **yscale(off fill) is drawn as off; twoway keeps the axis's space** [AX-7; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: C10 vs C11
  - Fix: Keep the border space when fill is given with off
- **Label sub-options twoway draws are named: labelminlen(), noshowlabels/showlabels, nopgmgrid/pgmgrid, style(); showlabels nolabels hides labels** [AX-8; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: _DASH_AXLABEL_HONOURED (dash_render.py:53662-53673); C05-C08, F9, F11
  - Fix: Map them to labels/grid; pad for labelminlen; read ticksetstyle files
- **xscale/yscale atanh, logit, log1m and style() are named and drawn linear** [AX-9, sil:SD-5; confirmed; tracked: closed GAPS3 D7 residual]
  - Evidence: scale.class:149-204; transform.class; C12-C17; GAPS3 D7 closed without them
  - Fix: Draw the transforms, or document them
- **Contradictory transforms in one copy (log nolog, atanh log) are accepted; twoway refuses r(198)** [AX-10; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: F1, F8
  - Fix: opts_exclusive within one copy, before the repeat merge
- **A log axis over data with non-positive values is ticked over positive values only (twoway's whole drawing is degenerate there)** [AX-11; partly; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: C20, G6; dash.sthlp:4662
  - Fix: Natscale over all finite values then drop <=0, or document
- **tstyle(), labstyle(), gstyle(), tlstyle(), glstyle() and nogextend are named** [AX-14; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:4568-4572; F12
  - Fix: Read the style files; draw nogextend with Grid bounds
- **Documented: xtick and xmtick share one tick length; out-of-range reflines are clipped from the margin; string/hex formats drawn %9.0g, dates past the browser range, margin fixed at build** [AX-15, AX-17, AX-19; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:4868-4870, 5098-5107, 4741-4751, 4818-4820
  - Fix: Optional custom glyphs; fix the past-Stata-dates tick rule
- **A date axis is kept where twoway labels Stata numbers (first layer lfit/qfit/ci/kdensity/lpoly/function/immediates); xscale(log) on a date axis is drawn linear** [AX-18, stat:PT-20, VF-12; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:4723-4731, 4815-4820
  - Fix: Label with numbers as twoway does; draw log date axes
- **xscale(range()) on a log axis adds a tick at the range end** [MS-14; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: B04 FixedTicker [1000..5000]; b2_log_range renders 20000
  - Fix: Do not add range ends to log default ticks; compare drawn_ticks
- **A graph whose only plot is on yaxis(2) is drawn with y gridlines; twoway draws none** [MS-12; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: _dash_lone_second_axes (dash_render.py:5003-5017); E14, C10
  - Fix: Keep axis k's grid default when renumbering
- **twoway's '<first title>...' x title (different x across y-axis groups, or upright plus turned layers) is not drawn** [MS-11, RH-17; confirmed; tracked: residual in closed BUGS P1.9a (horizontal case only)]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:39809-39841; E12, E13, C04, P36; test_horizontal.py:378 skips; dash.sthlp:3630-3632
  - Fix: Measure and apply the rule in both modes
- **ytitle(A) ytitle(B, suffix) is joined on one line; twoway draws two lines for axis titles** [MS-13; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_parse.mata:11170; E08, B17
  - Fix: Join axis-title suffix/prefix copies as separate lines
- **A right-hand y title reads top to bottom; twoway's reads upward (every economist graph, any yaxis(2))** [PIC-12; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: tw rotate(-90); axis_label_orientation never set
  - Fix: Set axis_label_orientation on right axes
- **%tC ticks are placed and labelled in leap-second-corrected time (22 s off in 2001)** [DG-6; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: T04/T05; no %tC row in stata_ticks_truth
  - Fix: Shift Stata's tick values by the leap seconds; label with %tC
- **Parenthesised dates holding blanks ((1 jan 2001)) are refused in tlabel(), tline() and tscale(range())** [DG-4; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_parse.mata:9308, 9512; DT04, DT05, DT09, DT20
  - Fix: Tokenise datelists binding parentheses
- **A non-number in a label list (.a, a bare word) is noted and drawn; twoway refuses 'invalid label specifier'** [DG-9, TR-8; confirmed (TR-8 partly); tracked: bare-word case documented; .a untracked]
  - Evidence: T11; value_truth xlabel_1..; _dash_axlabel_refusal (dash_parse.mata:2335-2392); dash.sthlp:4522-4523
  - Fix: Refuse in twoway's words
- **ylabel(minmax) on an empty scale draws Bokeh's default ticks; data spanning about ±8e307 give an OverflowError traceback (twoway gives r(199))** [DG-13, DG-11; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:42324-42340, 40316-40322; S07, X06
  - Fix: No ticks on an empty minmax axis; guard 10**z
- **Open entry: the natscale model misses 3 of 264 axes after a filter move or without embedded ticks** [plot:PT-13, AX-20, syn:SD-21, VF-9, BY-25, sil:SD-16; confirmed (the noted after-move exception); tracked: BUGS.md open natscale entry]
  - Evidence: BUGS.md:4139; test_natscale.py _KNOWN
  - Fix: Refit the model or call gdi natscale where Stata is available

### 10. Schemes, styles and figure geometry (major, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** Resolve if supported by Bokeh.  
**For planning:** As package 6.

Shipped schemes draw wrong (economist note missing, wrong widths), style words twoway reads are not read, and figure proportions follow a height band.

- **economist: a graph's note() is never painted (the Label targets a Title Node BokehJS does not resolve)** [PIC-4; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: d_tt_economist.html rendered headless; dash_render.py:54518-54527; dash.sthlp:6202-6205
  - Fix: Place it in a painted row; add a pixel test
- **economist widths: range, spike, dropline and pc lines at 1.2 (linewidth p) where twoway uses pother 0.8; the rarea outline at 0.8 centred where twoway uses parea 0.2 inside** [PIC-7, PIC-8; confirmed; tracked: untracked (BUGS S3 resolution wrong)]
  - Evidence: linestyle-p#other chain; linewidth_chain_truth lwt7; dash_render.py:12429-12446, 22978-23006
  - Fix: Route line widths through p#other and rarea through p#area; fix the test that pins the wrong key
- **Style-word abbreviations and names twoway reads are not read: compass2dir (nor, N, rig ...), tb_orientstyle (hor, vertic), clockdir names (top, bottom, left ...: title position(bottom) drawn above), gridringstyle (inside/outside; title ring(in) accepted where twoway refuses), anglestyle (v, rv, rh), connectstyle (st, up, no drawn direct); twoway-worded notes are invented for them, and justification(l)'s note is missing** [ST-1, ST-2, ST-3, ST-4, ST-5, ST-6, ST-11; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_parse.mata:11555-11590, 6844-6869, 10426-10435; dash_render.py:7538-7573, 54057, 39597-39598, 17650; class _unabbrev files; probe1 hashes
  - Fix: One reader per class following its _unabbrev rules; drop the invented notes; extend the fixtures
- **Leaf style files on the ado-path (linepattern, linewidth, symbolsize, gsize, intensity, anglestyle, margin) are not read** [ST-7; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: _STYLE_FILE_CLASSES (dash_render.py:10381-10382); probe1 G13, p3
  - Fix: Extend the style-file walk to the leaf classes
- **color-*.style and textboxstyle-*.style files are not read (mcolor(mine) draws black)** [ST-15, GW-18; confirmed; tracked: documented limit]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:6141-6153; BUGS.md:4385 (D36)
  - Fix: Read them with the other style classes
- **Scheme directives read but not drawn: clockdir p/p#, title textboxstyle, legend_col_gap, title clocks 2-4/8-10; a scheme name must not be a path; files over 1 MB or linked out are skipped** [ST-17, GW-18; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:6293-6303, 6366-6372, 6225; test_unread_scheme_directives.py
  - Fix: Draw the directives
- **A shipped-name scheme placed before BASE (adopath ++) is ignored; dash always uses its own translation** [ST-10; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: _resolve_path (dash_render.py:10542-10560); p3 F
  - Fix: Rank shipped translations at BASE's position
- **An unknown scheme() is refused r(198) where twoway notes and draws s2color** [ST-16, GW-17; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:1260-1264, 6305-6329
  - Fix: Note and draw s2color, or keep documented
- **The graph-region outline is not stroked by default, and the scheme's graph margin (3.5 units) is not drawn (Bokeh's 5 px min_border is used)** [GW-6, VF-7; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: region_truth D22; test_region_truth.py:194 skip; _dash_graph_margin (dash_render.py:40612-40650)
  - Fix: Stroke from the scheme's graphregion line; add the margin to min_border
- **graphregion's inner box and region lalign() are named; ifcolor() fills the whole border; the plot margin is a share of the whole graph** [ST-21, GW-19; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:4298-4311, 4329-4335
  - Fix: Draw an inner BoxAnnotation; compute the margin on the frame
- **Figure proportions: a lone graph fills the page width within a 160-560 px height band (2.14:1 at 1200 px where twoway is 1.667:1; xsize()/ysize()/aspectratio() alone change nothing); relative sizes are a fixed 2.88 pt (0.889x twoway under stcolor); fxsize()/fysize() shrink the cell** [VF-4, GW-20, sil:SD-21, VF-5, VF-11; confirmed; tracked: documented (Figure proportions)]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:518-519; dash.sthlp:5641-5723 (band values not stated)
  - Fix: Let the requested ratio win on a lone graph (or when xsize/ysize/aspectratio is given); scale the unit to the scheme's graphsize; state the band values
- **xsize()/ysize() with units (10cm, 3in, 200pt) are ignored with a note that leaks 'None'** [GW-1; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: region_options.sthlp:155-170; dash.ado:3753; float() at dash_render.py:27374, 27437, 27742
  - Fix: Parse in/pt/cm everywhere; measure out-of-range values
- **Colour edges: navy*52 intensity rule, blank stylelist element refused in dash's words** [ST-22; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: colour_grammar_truth opt_17; stylelist_truth R14; dash.sthlp:1316-1349
  - Fix: Match the clamp; reword the refusal
- **graph set window/svg fontface for plain text is not read (only stSans/stSerif/stMono tags)** [GW-10; confirmed (unverified: plain-text faces never measured); tracked: partly (closed BUGS entry, tags only)]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:7364-7387; dash_render.py:48452
  - Fix: Measure under changed fontface, then apply the default face

### 11. Accepted syntax and refusals (major, effort M)

**Decision (D40):** Make dash compatible with twoway on these issues.  
**For planning:** Fix all: accept what twoway accepts, refuse what twoway refuses, with twoway's rc and message.

Some valid twoway is refused (including repeated options twoway takes rightmost) and some invalid twoway is accepted.

- **Repeated options twoway takes rightmost are refused r(198): yvarlabel, xvarlabel, mlabprefix, mlabsuffix, lalign, mlabstyle (and yvarl) given twice, and repeated contour levels(), ccuts(), ccolors(), clabels(), format(), scolor/ecolor/crule/interp, clwidths/clpatterns** [MS-1, stat:PT-3; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: _dash_label_split (dash.ado:2517-2560, syntax at 2551); _dash_contour_split (2447-2499); twoway_zyx2view_parse.class:89-106; runs G01-G06, run3 #101-#119
  - Fix: Keep only the last copy before the combined syntax; keep refusing repeated flags; add TP-14a rows
- **p1()..p20(): p3()-p19() are refused, and p1(), p2() and p20() are noted and not drawn** [syn:SD-3; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twowaygraph_g.class:145-152; dash_parse.mata:8114-8115; p1.svg; v1out.log
  - Fix: Map p#(opts) onto the #th plot; regenerate the takes table over p1-p20
- **lowess m (twoway's documented minimum of mean) is refused** [stat:PT-7, syn:SD-6; confirmed; tracked: code comment only]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:1274, 1664-1671; lowm.log
  - Fix: On lowess, read a bare m as mean
- **bwi() is refused on lowess, kdensity, lpoly and lpolyci** [syn:SD-5; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:1266, 1318; abbrev_truth unprobeable.bwidth
  - Fix: Declare BWidth with twoway's minimum on those types
- **histogram accepts bins()+width() and two of density/fraction/frequency/percent; twoway refuses** [stat:PT-1, TR-7; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twoway__histogram_gen.ado:27-51; dash.ado:4788-4848; value_truth histogram_freqpct_1
  - Fix: Add both checks in twoway's words and order
- **density, fraction, frequency and percent are accepted on every type; twoway takes them only on histogram** [stat:PT-2; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:3772-3774; run3
  - Fix: Add them to the flag-applicability rule
- **contour levels() 501-1000 and 500+ ccuts() are accepted where twoway refuses at draw time; the help says twoway takes any integer** [stat:PT-5; confirmed; tracked: documented wrongly]
  - Evidence: lev2.log; dash.ado:2660-2665; dash.sthlp:596-598; extract_contour_truth.do:358 measured under nodraw
  - Fix: Bound at 500 levels and 499 cuts in twoway's words; re-measure drawn
- **contour abbreviations twoway documents (int(), i(), colorl, clp(), clw()) are refused or bound to other options** [stat:PT-13; confirmed; tracked: documented as full spellings required]
  - Evidence: twoway_contourline.sthlp:44-58; contour_truth abbrev.*; dash.sthlp:599-601
  - Fix: Resolve them to the contour options first
- **function subchar() is not honoured: subchar(t) fails r(111), and subchar(1t) is accepted with a note** [stat:PT-6; confirmed; tracked: help claim inaccurate]
  - Evidence: twoway_function_parse.class:55-71; dash.ado:10448 hard-codes x(x)
  - Fix: Validate it as a name and pass it as x()
- **lowess 'noweight weight' is accepted (twoway refuses), and bare weight is named although nothing changes** [stat:PT-8; confirmed; tracked: help Unsupported]
  - Evidence: twoway_lowess_parse.class:37-47; probe1 #77-#78
  - Fix: Treat the pair in the repeat check; take weight silently
- **Edge values twoway takes are refused: mspline bands(-1), fpfitci n(0), lfit n(-1)** [stat:PT-9; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twoway_mspline_parse.class:35; probe1 #114
  - Fix: Accept them as twoway does
- **bw() is accepted with a note on scatter/line/lfit and read as barwidth() on the bar family; twoway refuses it off the bandwidth types** [stat:PT-14, DT-13; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: _dash_fold_bw (dash.ado:2125-2145); dash.sthlp:3655-3658
  - Fix: Refuse it outside lowess, lpoly, lpolyci and kdensity
- **Two omit* options are accepted with a note; twoway refuses 'option omitanymissomitallmiss not allowed'** [basic:PT-8, syn:SD-10; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twoway_yxview_parse.class:238-243
  - Fix: Refuse in twoway's words
- **Value refusals use r(198) where twoway uses another code: base(abc) 4026, bin() 125/121/126, width() 125/121, start() 121, mlabel(make..) 101, rbar barwidth(.) 199, mlabangle(1e308) 111** [basic:PT-9, TR-9, stat:PT-10; confirmed; tracked: DROPIN P2.64 unmapped; partly documented dash.sthlp:1144-1146]
  - Evidence: value_truth.json; OV-42 and PS-06 pin dash's codes; DROPIN P2.64
  - Fix: Give each rule twoway's rc and line (the fixture holds them); flip OV-42 and PS-06
- **Values twoway draws are refused by dash's bounds: jitter(-3/150/abc), jitterseed(-1), barwidth(0/-1), aspectratio(-1), lwidth(-1) on scatter, msangle(400) on line, msize(1e400); lwidth(*40000) noted where twoway refuses** [basic:PT-13, DT-17, DT-18, TR-17; confirmed (barwidth(-1) is twoway's default sentinel); tracked: documented ('Some bounds are dash's own')]
  - Evidence: plaintypes4 C2-C10; Q05, Q06, Q12; dash.sthlp:1225-1250, 5871
  - Fix: Accept as twoway does (clamp or no-op); bound only where the element is drawn
- **Accepted where twoway refuses: kernel(GAUSSIAN), xsize(()), an empty-colorvar if, and about 35 dash-only words (ytitle2(), xlabel2(), mlg(), marker(), malpha(), jseed(), plotbgcolor(), hover(), table ...)** [DT-15, DT-16, syn:SD-18; confirmed; tracked: documented piecemeal]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:1251-1265, 4948-4953; p10out X08-X52
  - Fix: None needed for pasting twoway; optionally drop duplicate synonyms; list them in one place
- **ty() on lfitci/qfitci/fpfitci/lpolyci is refused; twoway refuses only the full word type** [syn:SD-7; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twoway_lfitci_parse.class:34-37; dash.ado:3248-3252
  - Fix: Refuse only exact type/recastas; measure what ty() draws
- **Factor-variable notation in by()/colorvar(c.) gives r(111) where twoway gives r(101); mlabel(45) gives r(111) where twoway gives r(198)** [syn:SD-8; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash.ado:4300-4321; F01-F05; v3
  - Fix: Refuse with twoway's rc and words
- **aspectratio() with xoverhangs/yoverhangs is accepted; twoway refuses r(198)** [GW-2; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twowaygraph_g.class:155-161
  - Fix: Refuse in twoway's words after the merge
- **maxaxes() is never checked: maxaxes(x)/(2.5) accepted, maxaxes(1) with yaxis(2) drawn** [GW-3; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twowaygraph_g.class:136
  - Fix: Validate it; refuse axis numbers above it with r(125)
- **fxsiz()/fysiz() (twoway's abbreviations) are noted and not applied** [GW-4; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: graph.ado:161; dash.ado:980, 1309
  - Fix: Declare FXSIZe/FYSIZe
- **title() with notitling is accepted with a note; twoway fails r(4018)** [sil:SD-8; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: twowaygraph_g.class:577-582
  - Fix: Refuse the pair as measured
- **Variables named __dash* give r(110); dash's working columns can give r(900) at c(maxvar)** [DT-19; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:211-243; Q11
  - Fix: Use tempnames or a frame for working columns

  Withdrawn under D40: stat:PT-4.

### 12. Statistical and computed layers (major, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** If there are zero observations after the if/in qualifiers, issue an error before the graph is created in Python, for every plot type, through a single function. If that differs from Stata, note it in the help; that note resolves it.  
**For planning:** Zero observations (DG-1, the contour crash, and every other type): one Stata-side check, run after each plot's and the graph's if/in are applied and before Python is called, refuses with an error. Where twoway draws something instead (for example an empty graph), the help documents the difference, and that is the resolution. The other items (rarea cmissing outlines, the contour subsample above 1,000 points, a contour with no surface, xlog/xtransform() on lfit, lpolyci at zero df, documented numeric limits, tie order) have no ruling in D40 and stay open.

Every Stata contour example is approximated, an empty contour crashes, and rarea outlines cross gaps.

- **contour over zero observations crashes with a Python traceback, r(7102)** [DG-1; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: map_contour (dash_render.py:24350-24425) passes NaN levels to fig.contour
  - Fix: Skip the band renderer and draw the titled key when there are no rows
- **rarea with cmissing(n) draws its bound outlines across the gaps** [DG-5; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: dash_render.py:22918-22998; d_M05.png; test_area_cmissing has no rarea outline test
  - Fix: Give the bound Lines their own NaN-broken columns
- **contour above 1,000 distinct points fits a seeded subsample on a 100x100 grid (every sandstone help example); a repeated node keeps the smallest z; a different polygon cutter** [TR-13, stat:PT-19, sil:SD-19; confirmed; tracked: documented; ROADMAP D4]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:565-592; dash_render.py:13992-14003
  - Fix: Run twoway__contour_gen at registration, or reproduce its grid at any size
- **A contour with no surface (two observations) draws no colour key** [DG-8; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: map_contour not-drawable branch; C01
  - Fix: Build the key from the levels anyway

  - Evidence: twoway_lfit_parse.class:75-88; sersets.log xtr
  - Fix: Route through _dash_fit_points with xtrans()
- **lpolyci at zero-df grid points: dash pinches to zero width where twoway draws a huge band that rescales y; the help calls it round-off** [stat:PT-16; confirmed; tracked: lpolyci_truth D4]
  - Evidence: lpolyci_truth a_d3 row 47; dash.sthlp:4032-4034

- **Documented numeric limits: lpoly bandwidth about 1% off at degree 6-7, degree above 7 differs (484.23 vs 400.94), exact-polynomial cases, fpfit date-time model choice, ndots thinning above 2,000,000** [DT-26; confirmed; tracked: documented]
  - Evidence: dash.sthlp:1166-1185, 3895-3916, 3970-3975, 3795-3796

- **Tied sort keys keep data order; twoway's order among ties is unstable (lowess drawn path, sort with by(), cmissing breaks)** [stat:PT-21, BY-30, DG-16; uncertain (stat:PT-21); partly (BY-30); confirmed (DG-16); tracked: documented]

### 13. Verification and test coverage (major, effort L)

**Decision (D40):** Top priority. Produce it so that it can be injected into the README to demonstrate the command's flexibility.  
**For planning:** Plan first. A broad twoway-vs-dash corpus comparison, for example every example in Stata's own twoway help files and g.pdf, run under both twoway and dash. Its output (command, twoway picture, dash picture, verdict) is generated in a form that can be injected into README.md as a gallery.

Most gaps above went unnoticed because no test compares dash's drawing with twoway over a broad corpus.

- **No picture comparison over a broad corpus (109 gr_example commands run here only once, by hand)** [TR-2; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: git grep gr_example is empty; twoway_corpus.do and conformance_corpus check rc only; test_mode_parity compares dash with dash
  - Fix: Commit a cert-tier harness: every gr_example in both tools, comparing ticks, labels, titles, keys, glyph counts/extents and colours, with a known-differences ledger

  - Evidence: test_option_validation.py:1395-1510 checks structure only
  - Fix: Parametrised rc and first-line check with a deliberate-differences list

  - Evidence: ROADMAP.md:5155; test_graph_text.py:1323-1336; conftest.py:195-208
  - Fix: Delete or convert TestBounds; run make test-all once and record it
- **Per-type by() drawing is pinned against twoway only for function (default range) and lpoly** [BY-23, BY-31; partly; tracked: GAPS3 K2 (partial)]
  - Evidence: U60-5 compares saved with served for five types; U46-04; test_lpoly_rot
  - Fix: Turn the 93-command byx probe into extract_by_types_truth.do and compare both modes per panel
- **Estimator combinations with no truth (all matched when measured): lfitci [pw], lpoly degree >=4, weighted kdensity boundary, combined lowess flags, histogram discrete [fw] ...** [stat:PT-23; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
  - Evidence: stat_truth keys; numeric.do

- **colorvar fixtures never compare key texts, order or frames; non-scatter options and interactions are unmeasured; disc_levels_price_4 passes vacuously** [CV-16; confirmed; tracked: untracked]
